└── onboarding/

                <version>${spring-boot.version}</version>
                <type>pom</type>

# MiComm — Multi-Module Maven Spring Boot Scaffold (Tenant Management Service First)

## Project Structure

```
micampaign/
├── pom.xml                              (parent POM)
├── common-lib/
│   ├── pom.xml
│   └── src/main/java/com/micomm/common/
│       ├── security/JwtTokenProvider.java
│       ├── security/JwtAuthFilter.java
│       ├── security/JwtProperties.java
│       └── dto/ApiResponse.java
│
└── tenant-management-service/
    ├── pom.xml
    └── src/main/java/com/micomm/tenantmgmt/
        ├── TenantManagementApplication.java
        ├── config/SecurityConfig.java
        ├── auth/
        │   ├── User.java
        │   ├── UserRole.java
        │   ├── UserRepository.java
        │   ├── RefreshToken.java
        │   ├── RefreshTokenRepository.java
        │   ├── AuthController.java
        │   ├── AuthService.java
        │   └── dto/ (LoginRequest, LoginResponse, RefreshRequest)
        ├── organization/
        │   ├── Organization.java
        │   ├── OrganizationRepository.java
        │   ├── OrganizationController.java
        │   └── OrganizationService.java
        └── onboarding/
            ├── TenantDatabase.java
            ├── TenantDatabaseRepository.java
            └── TenantOnboardingService.java
    └── src/main/resources/application.yml
```

## 1. Parent POM

````xml
// filepath: d:\micomm-git\micampaign\pom.xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0"
         xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
         xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0 http://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>com.micomm</groupId>
    <artifactId>micomm-platform</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>
    <packaging>pom</packaging>

    <properties>
        <java.version>17</java.version>
        <spring-boot.version>3.3.4</spring-boot.version>
        <maven.compiler.source>${java.version}</maven.compiler.source>
        <maven.compiler.target>${java.version}</maven.compiler.target>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
        <jjwt.version>0.12.6</jjwt.version>
    </properties>

    <modules>
        <module>common-lib</module>
        <module>tenant-management-service</module>
        <!-- <module>email-service</module> -->
        <!-- <module>sms-service</module> -->
    </modules>

    <dependencyManagement>
        <dependencies>
            <dependency>
                <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
                <artifactId>spring-boot-dependencies</artifactId>
                <version>${spring-boot.version}</version>
                <type>pom</type>
                <scope>import</scope>
            </dependency>
        </dependencies>
    </dependencyManagement>

</project>
`````

    <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>
    <packaging>pom</packaging>

    <properties>
        <java.version>17</java.version>
        <spring-boot.version>3.3.4</spring-boot.version>
        <maven.compiler.source>${java.version}</maven.compiler.source>
        <maven.compiler.target>${java.version}</maven.compiler.target>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>

    </modules>

    <dependencyManagement>
        <dependencies>
            <dependency>
                <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
                <artifactId>spring-boot-dependencies</artifactId>
                <version>${spring-boot.version}</version>
                <type>pom</type>
                <scope>import</scope>
            </dependency>
        </dependencies>
    </dependencyManagement>

</project>

         xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0 http://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>com.micomm</groupId>
    <artifactId>micomm-platform</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>
    <packaging>pom</packaging>

    <properties>
        <java.version>17</java.version>
        <spring-boot.version>3.3.4</spring-boot.version>
        <maven.compiler.source>${java.version}</maven.compiler.source>
        <maven.compiler.target>${java.version}</maven.compiler.target>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
        <jjwt.version>0.12.6</jjwt.version>
    </properties>

    <modules>
        <module>common-lib</module>
        <module>tenant-management-service</module>
        <!-- <module>email-service</module> -->
        <!-- <module>sms-service</module> -->
    </modules>

    <dependencyManagement>
        <dependencies>
            <dependency>
                <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
                <artifactId>spring-boot-dependencies</artifactId>
                <version>${spring-boot.version}</version>
                <type>pom</type>
                <scope>import</scope>
            </dependency>
        </dependencies>
    </dependencyManagement>

</project>
`````
